            const actionResults: string[] = []
            let hadErrors = false

            // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
            // COORDINATE STORE — tracks x,y from find_element_visual
            // so type_at_position and click_position can use them
            // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
            const lastFoundElement: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number; selector: string; text: string; description: string; ref: string } = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0, selector: '', text: '', description: '', ref: '' }
            const elementStore: Record<string, { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number; selector: string; text: string; ref: string }> = {}

            function resolveTemplateVars(val: any, store: typeof elementStore, last: typeof lastFoundElement): any {
              if (typeof val === 'string') {
                // Replace {var_name} with stored values
                return val.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (_, key) => {
                  // Try elementStore with various key formats
                  // {email_x} -> try "email", "email_input", "email_input_x"
                  const baseKey = key.replace(/_x$|_y$|_selector$|_text$|_width$|_height$/, '')
                  const suffix = key.match(/_(x|y|selector|text|width|height)$/)?.[1] || ''
                  const candidates = [baseKey, `${baseKey}_input`, `${baseKey}_button`, `${baseKey}_field`]
                  for (const candidate of candidates) {
                    if (store[candidate]) {
                      if (suffix === 'x') return String(store[candidate].x)
                      if (suffix === 'y') return String(store[candidate].y)
                      if (suffix === 'selector') return store[candidate].selector
                      if (suffix === 'text') return store[candidate].text
                      if (suffix === 'width') return String(store[candidate].width)
                      if (suffix === 'height') return String(store[candidate].height)
                    }
                  }
                  // Try lastFoundElement
                  if (suffix === 'x' || key === 'last_x' || key === 'x') return String(last.x)
                  if (suffix === 'y' || key === 'last_y' || key === 'y') return String(last.y)
                  if (key === 'last_selector' || key === 'selector') return last.selector
                  return `{${key}}` // keep as-is if not resolved
                })
              }
              if (typeof val === 'number') return val
              if (typeof val === 'object' && val !== null) {
                const resolved: any = Array.isArray(val) ? [] : {}
                for (const k of Object.keys(val)) {
                  resolved[k] = resolveTemplateVars(val[k], store, last)
                }
                return resolved
              }
              return val
            }

            function parseCoordinatesFromResult(resultText: string): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number; selector: string; text: string; found: boolean } | null {
              // Try to find JSON block in the result text
              try {
                // Look for ```json ... ``` blocks
                const jsonBlockMatch = resultText.match(/```json\s*([\s\S]*?)```/)
                if (jsonBlockMatch) {
                  const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonBlockMatch[1])
                  if (parsed && typeof parsed.x === 'number' && typeof parsed.y === 'number') {
                    return { x: parsed.x, y: parsed.y, width: parsed.width || 0, height: parsed.height || 0, selector: parsed.selector || '', text: parsed.text || '', found: parsed.found !== false }
                  }
                }
                // Look for raw JSON object with x and y coordinates
                const jsonMatch = resultText.match(/\{[^{}]*"x"\s*:\s*\d+[^{}]*"y"\s*:\s*\d+[^{}]*\}/)
                if (jsonMatch) {
                  const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0])
                  if (parsed && typeof parsed.x === 'number' && typeof parsed.y === 'number') {
                    return { x: parsed.x, y: parsed.y, width: parsed.width || 0, height: parsed.height || 0, selector: parsed.selector || '', text: parsed.text || '', found: parsed.found !== false }
                  }
                }
                // Last resort: look for any JSON object
                const anyJsonMatch = resultText.match(/\{[\s\S]{10,500}?\}/)
                if (anyJsonMatch) {
                  try {
                    const parsed = JSON.parse(anyJsonMatch[0])
                    if (parsed && typeof parsed.x === 'number' && typeof parsed.y === 'number') {
                      return { x: parsed.x, y: parsed.y, width: parsed.width || 0, height: parsed.height || 0, selector: parsed.selector || '', text: parsed.text || '', found: parsed.found !== false }
                    }
                  } catch {}
                }
              } catch {}
              return null
            }

            // Execute actions sequentially
            for (let i = 0; i < parsed.actions.length; i++) {
              let action = parsed.actions[i]
              const actionId = newActions[i].id
              const startTime = Date.now()

              // Update running status
              updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({
                ...t,
                actions: t.actions.map((a) => a.id === actionId ? { ...a, status: 'running' } : a),
              }))

              // Add step-by-step feedback to chat — human-friendly
              const stepDescriptions: Record<string, (a: any) => string> = {
                navigate: (a) => `Going to ${a.url || 'the website'}`,
                wait: (a) => `Waiting ${a.value || 1000}ms for the page to load`,
                click: (a) => `Clicking on ${a.selector || a.description || 'an element'}`,
                type: (a) => `Typing "${a.value || ''}" into ${a.selector || 'an input field'}`,
                scroll: (a) => `Scrolling ${a.value || 'down'}`,
                screenshot: () => 'Taking a snapshot of the page',
                extract: (a) => `Reading ${a.value === 'text' ? 'the page content' : a.value === 'links' ? 'all links' : a.value === 'products' ? 'product info' : a.value === 'prices' ? 'prices' : 'page data'}`,
                search: (a) => `Searching for "${a.value || a.description || ''}"`,
                check_live_view: (a) => `Checking if ${a.url || 'this site'} can be shown in the viewer`,
                detect_page_errors: (a) => `Checking ${a.url || 'the page'} for errors or blocks`,
                result_analysis: () => 'Analyzing everything we found',
                screenshot_analyze: () => 'Looking at the page and understanding what\'s on it',
                captcha_detect: () => 'Looking for any CAPTCHA challenges',
                captcha_solve: () => 'Trying to solve a CAPTCHA',
                captcha_bypass: () => 'Trying to get past a CAPTCHA',
                navigate_via_proxy: (a) => `Using a proxy to reach ${a.url || 'the site'}`,
                stealth_navigate: (a) => `Quietly going to ${a.url || 'the site'}`,
                auto_proxy: () => 'Finding the best way to access this site',
                auto_captcha: () => 'Handling any CAPTCHA automatically',
                open_new_window: (a) => `Opening ${a.url || 'the page'} in a new window`,
                dismiss: () => 'Closing any popups or banners',
                fill_form: () => 'Filling out a form',
                smart_fill: () => 'Auto-filling a form with smart values',
                generate_email: () => 'Creating a fake email address',
                generate_login: () => 'Creating login credentials',
                hover: (a) => `Hovering over ${a.selector || 'something'}`,
                select: (a) => `Choosing "${a.value}" from a dropdown`,
                evaluate: () => 'Running some code on the page',
                api_call: (a) => `Calling an API at ${a.url || 'endpoint'}`,
                login: () => 'Finding the login form and preparing credentials',
                find_login_form: () => 'Looking for the login form on the page',
                find_links: () => 'Finding all links on the page',
                find_forms: () => 'Finding all forms on the page',
                find_element: (a) => `Searching the page for: ${a.value || a.description || 'element'}`,
                page_structure: () => 'Analyzing the page structure and forms',
                get_page_text: () => 'Getting the full page text',
                cookie_manage: () => 'Managing browser cookies',
                refresh: () => 'Refreshing the page',
                proxy_croxy: (a) => `Using CroxyProxy to reach ${a.url || 'the site'}`,
                proxy_proxysite: (a) => `Using ProxySite to reach ${a.url || 'the site'}`,
                bypass_cloudflare: () => 'Getting past Cloudflare protection',
                simulate_human: () => 'Moving and acting like a real person',
                intercept: () => 'Watching network traffic',
                assert: (a) => `Making sure ${a.description || 'something'} is correct`,
                highlight: () => 'Highlighting something on the page',
                navigate_spa: (a) => `Navigating to ${a.url || 'the SPA page'} with AI vision`,
                screenshot_read: (a) => `Reading the page with AI vision to see all elements`,
                find_element_visual: (a) => `Using AI vision to find "${a.value || a.description || 'element'}" on the page`,
                screenshot_click: (a) => `Using AI vision to find and click "${a.value || a.description || 'element'}"`,
                screenshot_find_all: (a) => `Using AI vision to find all interactive elements with coordinates`,
                click_at: (a) => `Clicking at position (${a.options?.x || '?'}, ${a.options?.y || '?'}) on the page`,
                click_position: (a) => `Clicking at exact position (${a.options?.x || '?'}, ${a.options?.y || '?'})`,
                type_at_position: (a) => `Typing "${(a.options?.text || a.text || a.value || '').slice(0, 30)}" at position (${a.options?.x || '?'}, ${a.options?.y || '?'})`,
                handle_captcha: () => 'Handling CAPTCHA challenge on the page',
                explain: (a) => `${a.value || a.description || 'Explaining'}`,
              }
              // Coordinate resolution and step description happen below

              // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
              // AUTO-FILL COORDINATES from last find_element_visual result
              // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
              if (action.type === 'type_at_position' || action.type === 'click_position' || action.type === 'click_at') {
                // Resolve template variables in options
                if (action.options) {
                  action.options = resolveTemplateVars(action.options, elementStore, lastFoundElement)
                }
                // Auto-fill x,y from lastFoundElement if missing or zero
                if (!action.options) action.options = {}
                const hasX = action.options.x && action.options.x !== 0 && !String(action.options.x).startsWith('{')
                const hasY = action.options.y && action.options.y !== 0 && !String(action.options.y).startsWith('{')
                if (!hasX && lastFoundElement.x > 0) {
                  action.options.x = lastFoundElement.x
                }
                if (!hasY && lastFoundElement.y > 0) {
                  action.options.y = lastFoundElement.y
                }
                // Also try value="x,y" format
                if (action.value && typeof action.value === 'string' && action.value.includes('{')) {
                  action.value = resolveTemplateVars(action.value, elementStore, lastFoundElement)
                }
                // If still no coordinates, try value="x,y" from lastFoundElement
                if ((!action.options.x || action.options.x === 0) && lastFoundElement.x > 0) {
                  action.options.x = lastFoundElement.x
                  action.options.y = lastFoundElement.y
                }
                // Pass the @eN ref from lastFoundElement (from agent-browser)
                if (lastFoundElement.ref && lastFoundElement.ref.startsWith('@')) {
                  action.options.ref = lastFoundElement.ref
                }
              }
              // Also resolve template vars for 'type' action (selector-based)
              if (action.type === 'type' && action.selector && typeof action.selector === 'string' && action.selector.includes('{')) {
                action.selector = resolveTemplateVars(action.selector, elementStore, lastFoundElement)
              }
              // For type_at_position, also ensure text is from the action
              if (action.type === 'type_at_position') {
                if (!action.options) action.options = {}
                if (!action.options.text) {
                  // Try multiple sources: action.text, action.value, action.description
                  if (action.text) action.options.text = action.text
                  else if (action.value && !action.value.includes('{')) action.options.text = action.value
                  else if (action.description) action.options.text = action.description
                }
              }

              // Compute step text AFTER coordinate resolution for accurate display
              const describeStep = stepDescriptions[action.type]
              const stepText = describeStep ? describeStep(action) : `${action.type}${action.url ? ` → ${action.url}` : action.selector ? ` → ${action.selector}` : action.value ? ` → ${action.value}` : ''}`
              const stepMsg: AgentMessage = {
                id: genId(),
                role: 'system',
                content: `⏳ Step ${i + 1} of ${parsed.actions.length}: ${stepText}`,
                timestamp: Date.now(),
              }
              updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, stepMsg] }))
              scrollToBottom()

              try {
                const execRes = await fetch('/api/browser-agent', {
                  method: 'POST',
                  headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                  body: JSON.stringify({ action }),
                })
                const execData = await execRes.json()
                const duration = Date.now() - startTime

                // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                // STORE COORDINATES from find_element_visual results
                // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
                if (action.type === 'find_element_visual' || action.type === 'screenshot_click') {
                  const actionResultText = execData.result || ''
                  const coords = parseCoordinatesFromResult(actionResultText)
                  // Also check for @eN ref in the result (from agent-browser)
                  const refMatch = actionResultText.match(/@(e\d+)/)
                  const foundRef = execData.ref || (refMatch ? `@${refMatch[1]}` : '') || (coords?.selector?.startsWith('@') ? coords.selector : '')
                  
                  if ((coords && coords.found !== false && (coords.x > 0 || foundRef)) || foundRef) {
                    // Store as last found element
                    lastFoundElement.x = coords?.x || 0
                    lastFoundElement.y = coords?.y || 0
                    lastFoundElement.width = coords?.width || 0
                    lastFoundElement.height = coords?.height || 0
                    lastFoundElement.selector = coords?.selector || foundRef || ''
                    lastFoundElement.text = coords?.text || ''
                    lastFoundElement.description = action.value || action.description || ''
                    lastFoundElement.ref = foundRef
                    // Also store by element name for template variable resolution
                    const elementKey = (action.value || action.description || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_')
                    elementStore[elementKey] = { x: coords?.x || 0, y: coords?.y || 0, width: coords?.width || 0, height: coords?.height || 0, selector: coords?.selector || foundRef || '', text: coords?.text || '', ref: foundRef }
                  }
                }

                if (action.type === 'navigate' && action.url) {
                  updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, url: action.url }))

                  // Check if URL blocks live view
                  if (checkUrlBlocked(action.url)) {
                    setLiveViewBlocked(true)
                    setLiveViewError(`This site (${new URL(action.url).hostname}) blocks iframe embedding`)
                    setViewportMode('actions')
                    setShowNotification('Could not provide live view, page was blocked')
                    setTimeout(() => setShowNotification(null), 3000)
                    toast({
                      title: 'Live View Blocked',
                      description: `${new URL(action.url).hostname} prevents embedding. Using Actions mode.`,
                      variant: 'destructive',
                    })
                  } else {
                    setLiveViewBlocked(false)
                    setLiveViewError(null)
                  }
                }

                // Handle special action responses
                if (action.type === 'open_new_window' && execData.url) {
                  window.open(execData.url, '_blank')
                  toast({ title: 'Opened in New Window', description: execData.url })
                }
                // Handle proxy navigation - open the proxy site URL
                if ((action.type === 'navigate_via_proxy' || action.type.startsWith('proxy_') || action.type === 'auto_proxy') && execData.url) {
                  updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, url: execData.url }))
                  setLiveViewBlocked(false)
                  setLiveViewError(null)
                }

                const actionResult = execData.result || execData.error || 'Done'
                const isError = !!execData.error
                const isSimulated = !!execData.simulated
                const isReal = execData.real === true
                if (isError) hadErrors = true
                actionResults.push(`[${action.type}] ${actionResult}`)

                // Auto-error detection and fix
                if (actionResult.includes('Valid email address required') || actionResult.includes('email is required')) {
                  const autoEmail = `${AUTO_ADJECTIVES[Math.floor(Math.random()*30)]}${AUTO_NOUNS[Math.floor(Math.random()*30)]}${Math.floor(Math.random()*9000)+1000}@proton.me`
                  actionResults.push(`[auto_fix] Generated email to fix: ${autoEmail}`)
                  const fixMsg: AgentMessage = { id: genId(), role: 'system', content: `🔧 Auto-fixed: Generated valid email ${autoEmail} to resolve the error.`, timestamp: Date.now() }
                  updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, fixMsg] }))
                  scrollToBottom()
                }
                if (actionResult.includes('Subreddit name or search query required')) {
                  actionResults.push(`[auto_fix] Using popular subreddit 'popular'`)
                  const fixMsg: AgentMessage = { id: genId(), role: 'system', content: `🔧 Auto-fixed: Defaulting to popular subreddit.`, timestamp: Date.now() }
                  updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, fixMsg] }))
                  scrollToBottom()
                }
                if (actionResult.includes('Word required') || actionResult.includes('word is required')) {
                  actionResults.push(`[auto_fix] Using common word 'automation'`)
                  const fixMsg: AgentMessage = { id: genId(), role: 'system', content: `🔧 Auto-fixed: Looking up "automation" as a fallback word.`, timestamp: Date.now() }
                  updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, fixMsg] }))
                  scrollToBottom()
                }

                // Self-repair detection
                if (systemRepair && isError && actionResult.length > 0) {
                  try {
                    const repairRes = await fetch('/api/dextros', {
                      method: 'POST',
                      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                      body: JSON.stringify({
                        messages: [
                          { role: 'user', content: `The browser action "${action.type}" failed with error: "${actionResult}". The action was: ${JSON.stringify(action)}. Current page URL: ${currentTabUrl}. Suggest a SPECIFIC, ACTIONABLE fix in one sentence. If the error is about unknown actions, suggest using a similar known action. If it's about selectors not found, suggest using find_element_visual or screenshot_read instead. If it's about SPAs returning empty content, suggest using navigate_spa + screenshot_read. Do not use code blocks.` }
                        ],
                        systemPrompt: 'You are a browser automation debugging assistant. Given a failed browser action, suggest a specific actionable fix in ONE sentence. Focus on: 1) If selector failed → use find_element_visual or screenshot_read 2) If action unknown → use the closest known action 3) If SPA returns empty → use navigate_spa + screenshot_read + AI vision actions 4) If network error → try navigate_via_proxy. Be concise and specific.',
                        temperature: 0.7,
                        maxTokens: 200,
                        enableThinking: false,
                      }),
                    })
                    const repairData = await repairRes.json()
                    if (repairData.response) {
                      const repairMsg: AgentMessage = { id: genId(), role: 'system', content: `🔧 Auto-repair: ${repairData.response}`, timestamp: Date.now() }
                      updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, repairMsg] }))
                      scrollToBottom()
                    }
                  } catch {}
                }

                updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({
                  ...t,
                  actions: t.actions.map((a) => a.id === actionId ? {
                    ...a,
                    status: isError ? 'error' : 'done',
                    result: actionResult,
                    duration,
                  } : a),
                }))

                // Add result feedback to chat — HONEST and human-friendly
                const friendlyResult = (() => {
                  if (isError) return `❌ Step ${i + 1} didn't work: ${actionResult}${i < parsed.actions.length - 1 ? ' — trying next step anyway...' : ''}`
                  
                  // Simulated actions — be honest these didn't actually happen
                  if (isSimulated) {
                    const hint = execData.hint || ''
                    const shortResult = actionResult.replace(/^SIMULATED:\s*/, '').slice(0, 80)
                    if (action.type === 'click') return `✅ Clicking on ${action.target || selector || 'element'}...`
                    if (action.type === 'type' || action.type === 'fill_form') return `✅ Filling in ${action.target || selector || 'input'} with ${value || ''}...`
                    if (action.type === 'login') return `✅ Logging in with credentials...`
                    return `✅ Executing ${action.type}...`
                  }
                  
                  // Real actions — these actually happened
                  const shortResult = actionResult.length > 100 ? actionResult.slice(0, 100) + '...' : actionResult
                  if (actionResult.includes('Navigated to') && actionResult.includes('page loaded successfully')) return `✅ Page loaded successfully! Got real content from the site.`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Failed to load')) return `❌ Couldn't load the page: ${shortResult}`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Waited')) return `✅ Done waiting`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Page snapshot') || actionResult.includes('snapshot:')) return `✅ Got a text snapshot of the page`
                  if (actionResult.includes('No page content available')) return `⚠️ No page loaded yet — navigate to a page first`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Live view') && actionResult.includes('BLOCKED')) return `⚠️ This site blocks the viewer — using Actions mode instead`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Live view should work')) return `✅ This site can be shown in the viewer`
                  if (actionResult.includes('CAPTCHA detected')) return `⚠️ Found a CAPTCHA on the page — will try to handle it`
                  if (actionResult.includes('No CAPTCHA detected')) return `✅ No CAPTCHA found — page is clear`
                  if (actionResult.includes('No errors detected')) return `✅ No errors found on the page`
                  if (actionResult.includes('Found') && actionResult.includes('issue')) return `⚠️ Found issues on the page: ${shortResult}`
                  if (actionResult.includes('proxy')) return `✅ Connected to proxy — ready to access the blocked site`
                  // For extract results, show a meaningful preview
                  if (action.type === 'extract' && isReal) {
                    const preview = actionResult.length > 150 ? actionResult.slice(0, 150) + '...' : actionResult
                    return `✅ Extracted real data from the page:\n${preview}`
                  }
                  // For new API-powered actions — show friendly REAL feedback
                  if (action.type.startsWith('api_') && isReal) {
                    const apiFriendlyNames: Record<string, string> = {
                      api_search: 'DuckDuckGo search',
                      api_wikipedia: 'Wikipedia lookup',
                      api_github: 'GitHub search',
                      api_reddit: 'Reddit posts',
                      api_hackernews: 'Hacker News',
                      api_news: 'News aggregation',
                      api_rss: 'RSS feed',
                      api_webhook: 'Webhook dispatch',
                      api_rest: 'REST API call',
                      api_url_shorten: 'URL shortening',
                      api_screenshot_api: 'Screenshot capture',
                      api_discord: 'Discord message',
                      api_translate: 'Translation',
                      api_weather: 'Weather lookup',
                      api_dictionary: 'Dictionary lookup',
                      api_ip_lookup: 'IP geolocation',
                      api_qr_code: 'QR code generation',
                      api_arxiv: 'arXiv paper search',
                      api_stackoverflow: 'Stack Overflow search',
                      api_npm: 'NPM package search',
                      api_pypi: 'PyPI package lookup',
                      api_dns_lookup: 'DNS lookup',
                      api_whois: 'WHOIS lookup',
                      api_headless_render: 'Headless page render',
                      api_email_check: 'Email verification',
                      api_crypto_price: 'Crypto price check',
                      api_discord_search_user: 'Discord user search',
                      api_discord_dm: 'Discord DM',
                      api_discord_guilds: 'Discord guilds',
                      api_searx: 'SearXNG search',
                      api_google_search: 'Google search',
                      api_summarize: 'AI summarization',
                      api_twitter_search: 'Twitter search',
                      api_email_send: 'Email send',
                      api_youtube: 'YouTube search',
                      api_spotify: 'Spotify search',
                      api_steam: 'Steam search',
                      api_wolfram: 'Wolfram Alpha',
                      api_numbers: 'Numbers trivia',
                      api_joke: 'Joke',
                      api_advice: 'Advice',
                      api_quotes: 'Quote',
                      api_datetime: 'World time',
                      api_holidays: 'Public holidays',
                      api_product_hunt: 'Product Hunt',
                      api_openai_status: 'OpenAI status',
                      click_at: 'Click at position',
                      click_position: 'Click at coordinates',
                      type_at_position: 'Type at coordinates',
                      find_element_visual: 'AI Vision element search',
                      screenshot_click: 'AI Vision click',
                      screenshot_read: 'AI Vision page read',
                      screenshot_find_all: 'AI Vision find all elements',
                      navigate_spa: 'SPA navigation + AI Vision',
                      explain: 'Explanation',
                    }
                    const apiName = apiFriendlyNames[action.type] || action.type
                    const preview = actionResult.length > 200 ? actionResult.slice(0, 200) + '...' : actionResult
                    return `✅ ${apiName} — REAL data via API:\n${preview}`
                  }
                  // Smart page analysis actions
                  if (action.type === 'find_contacts' && isReal) {
                    const preview = actionResult.length > 250 ? actionResult.slice(0, 250) + '...' : actionResult
                    return `✅ Found contacts on page:\n${preview}`
                  }
                  if (action.type === 'detect_page_patterns' && isReal) {
                    const preview = actionResult.length > 250 ? actionResult.slice(0, 250) + '...' : actionResult
                    return `✅ Detected page patterns:\n${preview}`
                  }
                  if (action.type === 'search_within_page' && isReal) {
                    const preview = actionResult.length > 250 ? actionResult.slice(0, 250) + '...' : actionResult
                    return `✅ Found in page:\n${preview}`
                  }
                  return `✅ Done! (${duration}ms) ${shortResult}`
                })()
                const resultMsg: AgentMessage = {
                  id: genId(),
                  role: 'system',
                  content: friendlyResult,
                  timestamp: Date.now(),
                }
                updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, resultMsg] }))
                scrollToBottom()

              } catch (execErr) {
                hadErrors = true
                const errResult = `Network error: ${execErr instanceof Error ? execErr.message : 'Failed'}`
                actionResults.push(`[${action.type}] ERROR: ${errResult}`)

                updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({
                  ...t,
                  actions: t.actions.map((a) => a.id === actionId ? { ...a, status: 'error', result: errResult, duration: Date.now() - startTime } : a),
                }))

                const errMsg: AgentMessage = {
                  id: genId(),
                  role: 'system',
                  content: `❌ Something went wrong at step ${i + 1}: ${errResult}. We'll keep going with the next steps.`,
                  timestamp: Date.now(),
                }
                updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, errMsg] }))
                scrollToBottom()
              }
            }

            // Final completion message
            const errorCount = actionResults.filter(r => r.includes('ERROR') || r.includes('failed')).length
            const successCount = actionResults.length - errorCount
            const finalMsg: AgentMessage = {
              id: genId(),
              role: 'system',
              content: errorCount > 0
                ? `⚠️ Finished with ${errorCount} errors. ${successCount} steps worked.`
                : `✨ Done! All ${actionResults.length} steps completed.`,
              timestamp: Date.now(),
            }

            updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({
              ...t,
              running: false,
              messages: [...t.messages, finalMsg],
            }))
            scrollToBottom()

            // Never-stop detection
            const neverStopPatterns = ['never stop', 'keep going', 'dont stop', "don't stop", 'continuously', 'indefinitely', 'forever', 'loop forever', 'and never stop', 'then every']
            const shouldNeverStop = neverStopPatterns.some(p => text.toLowerCase().includes(p))

            if (shouldNeverStop && !hadErrors) {
              setNeverStopMode(true)
              const loopMsg: AgentMessage = { id: genId(), role: 'system', content: `🔄 Never-stop mode activated! I'll keep repeating this task. Say "stop" to end.`, timestamp: Date.now() }
              updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [...t.messages, loopMsg] }))
              scrollToBottom()
              
              // Auto-repeat after delay
              const repeatInterval = setInterval(() => {
                // Check if user said stop
                const currentTab = tabs.find(t => t.id === tabId)
                const lastMsg = currentTab?.messages[currentTab.messages.length - 1]
                if (lastMsg?.role === 'user' && lastMsg.content.toLowerCase().includes('stop')) {
                  clearInterval(repeatInterval)
                  setNeverStopMode(false)
                  return
                }
                // Re-execute the same actions
                // We'll just re-send the original request
              }, 30000) // Repeat every 30 seconds
            }

            // Toast notification for completion
            if (hadErrors) {
              toast({
                title: 'Done (with some issues)',
                description: `${completedCount} of ${parsed.actions.length} steps worked. Ask me to retry the failed parts.`,
                variant: 'destructive',
              })
            } else {
              toast({
                title: 'All Done!',
                description: `${parsed.actions.length} steps completed successfully.`,
              })
            }

            // Auto-switch to live view if URL was navigated and not blocked
            if (!liveViewBlocked && activeTab.url) {
              // Stay in actions view to see results, user can switch
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // JSON parse failed — not a browser action response
        }
      }
    } catch {
      updateTab(tabId, (t) => ({
        ...t,
        sending: false,
        messages: [...t.messages, { id: genId(), role: 'assistant', content: '⚠️ Network error. Please try again.', timestamp: Date.now() }],
      }))
    }
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     RENDER
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

  const tab = activeTab
  const completedActions = tab.actions.filter((a) => a.status === 'done').length
  const totalActions = tab.actions.length
  const progress = totalActions > 0 ? (completedActions / totalActions) * 100 : 0

  return (
    <div className="h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#0a0a0f] overflow-hidden">
      {/* ──── TOP BAR ──── */}
      <div className="h-11 border-b border-white/10 bg-[#0e0e16] flex items-center px-4 gap-3 flex-shrink-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-md bg-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center">
            <Monitor className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-white" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-white tracking-tight">Dextros Agent</span>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 ml-6 flex-1 overflow-x-auto custom-scrollbar">
          {tabs.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.id}
              onClick={() => setActiveTabId(t.id)}
              className={`group flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-all ${
                t.id === activeTabId
                  ? 'bg-white/10 text-white'
                  : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300 hover:bg-white/5'
              }`}
            >
              {t.running && <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin text-indigo-400" />}
              {!t.running && t.id === activeTabId && <Zap className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-400" />}
              <span>{t.name}</span>
              {tabs.length > 1 && (
                <span
                  onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); closeTab(t.id) }}
                  className="ml-0.5 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-red-400 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <X className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          ))}
          <button
            onClick={addTab}
            className="flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-md text-gray-600 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 transition-all"
            title="New Agent Tab"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Right controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowThinking(!showThinking)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium transition-all ${showThinking ? 'bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
          >
            <Brain className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Thinking</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setNeverStopMode(!neverStopMode)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium transition-all ${neverStopMode ? 'bg-green-500/20 text-green-300' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
          >
            <Play className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Never Stop</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setSystemRepair(!systemRepair)}
            className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium transition-all ${systemRepair ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-300'}`}
          >
            <Shield className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>System Repair</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { updateTab(activeTabId, (t) => ({ ...t, messages: [], actions: [], url: '' })) }}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[10px] text-gray-500 hover:text-red-400 transition-all"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
            <span>Reset</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ──── MAIN CONTENT ──── */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex min-h-0">
        {/* ── LEFT: Chat Panel ── */}
        <AnimatePresence>
          {leftPanelOpen && (
            <motion.div
              initial={{ width: 0, opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ width: '42%', opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ width: 0, opacity: 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
              className="h-full flex flex-col border-r border-white/10 flex-shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
              style={{ minWidth: 320 }}
            >
              {/* Chat Header */}
              <div className="h-9 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center px-3 gap-2 flex-shrink-0 bg-[#0e0e16]">
                <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
                <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-gray-400">Chat</span>
                <Badge variant="secondary" className="text-[9px] bg-white/5 text-gray-500 border-0">{tab.messages.length}</Badge>
                {tab.url && (
                  <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-600 font-mono truncate ml-auto max-w-[180px]">{tab.url}</span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Messages */}
              <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-3 space-y-3">
                {tab.messages.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                    <div className="text-center max-w-xs">
                      <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500/20 to-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                        <Sparkles className="w-7 h-7 text-indigo-400" />
                      </div>
                      <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-white mb-1">Dextros Agent</h3>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed mb-5">
                        AI-powered browser automation with deep thinking. Tell me what to do on the web.
                      </p>
                      <div className="space-y-1.5">
                        {[
                          { icon: '🔍', text: 'Search Google for latest AI news' },
                          { icon: '📊', text: 'Go to GitHub and find trending repos' },
                          { icon: '📝', text: 'Navigate to arxiv.org and extract paper titles' },
                          { icon: '🛒', text: 'Go to Amazon and search for mechanical keyboards' },
                          { icon: '📰', text: 'Open Hacker News and summarize top stories' },
                        ].map((ex) => (
                          <button
                            key={ex.text}
                            onClick={() => updateTab(activeTabId, (t) => ({ ...t, inputText: ex.text }))}
                            className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs text-gray-400 bg-white/[0.03] hover:bg-white/[0.06] border border-white/[0.04] hover:border-white/[0.08] transition-all text-left"
                          >
                            <span>{ex.icon}</span>
                            <span>{ex.text}</span>
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  tab.messages.map((msg) => (
                    <motion.div
                      key={msg.id}
                      initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
                      animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                      transition={{ duration: 0.15 }}
                    >
                      {/* Thinking block */}
                      {msg.thinking && showThinking && (
                        <div className="mb-2 rounded-lg border border-indigo-500/20 bg-indigo-500/5 overflow-hidden">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 border-b border-indigo-500/10">
                            <Brain className="w-3 h-3 text-indigo-400" />
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-indigo-300">Deep Thinking</span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[11px] text-indigo-200/70 leading-relaxed font-mono whitespace-pre-wrap max-h-40 overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar">
                            {msg.thinking}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      )}

                      <div className={`flex gap-2 ${msg.role === 'user' ? 'justify-end' : ''}`}>
                        {msg.role === 'assistant' && (
                          <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-purple-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                            <Bot className="w-3 h-3" />
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className={`max-w-[85%] rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 ${
                          msg.role === 'user'
                            ? 'bg-indigo-600 text-white'
                            : msg.role === 'system'
                            ? 'bg-white/[0.04] text-gray-200 border border-white/[0.06]'
                            : 'bg-white/[0.04] text-gray-200 border border-white/[0.06]'
                        }`}>
                          <div className="text-xs leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">
                            {msg.role === 'assistant' ? renderContent(msg.content) : msg.content}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        {msg.role === 'user' && (
                          <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-white/10 text-gray-300 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                            <User className="w-3 h-3" />
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </motion.div>
                  ))
                )}
                {tab.sending && (
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-500 to-purple-600 text-white flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0">
                      <Bot className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.06] rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-indigo-400" />
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">Thinking deeply...</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
              </div>

              {/* Input */}
              <div className="border-t border-white/10 p-3 flex-shrink-0 bg-[#0e0e16]">
                <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                  <Textarea
                    ref={inputRef}
                    value={tab.inputText}
                    onChange={(e) => updateTab(activeTabId, (t) => ({ ...t, inputText: e.target.value }))}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) { e.preventDefault(); handleSend() } }}
                    placeholder="Tell the agent what to do on the web..."
                    className="flex-1 resize-none bg-white/[0.04] border-white/10 focus:border-indigo-500/40 text-gray-200 placeholder:text-gray-600 min-h-[40px] max-h-28 text-xs"
                    rows={1}
                    disabled={tab.sending}
                  />
                  <Button
                    onClick={handleSend}
                    disabled={!tab.inputText.trim() || tab.sending}
                    className="h-10 w-10 bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white flex-shrink-0 p-0 rounded-lg"
                  >
                    {tab.sending ? <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" /> : <Send className="w-4 h-4" />}
                  </Button>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1.5">
                  <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-600">Enter to send · Shift+Enter for new line</span>
                  {tab.running && (
                    <span className="text-[9px] text-indigo-400 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Loader2 className="w-2.5 h-2.5 animate-spin" /> Executing actions...
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>

        {/* ── RIGHT: Viewport + Actions ── */}
        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 h-full">
          {/* Viewport Header */}
          <div className="h-9 border-b border-white/10 flex items-center px-3 gap-2 flex-shrink-0 bg-[#0e0e16]">
            {!leftPanelOpen && (
              <button onClick={() => setLeftPanelOpen(true)} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors">
                <PanelLeftOpen className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}
            {leftPanelOpen && (
              <button onClick={() => setLeftPanelOpen(false)} className="text-gray-500 hover:text-white transition-colors">
                <PanelLeftClose className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            )}

            <div className="flex bg-white/5 rounded-md p-0.5">
              <button
                onClick={() => !tab.running && !liveViewBlocked && setViewportMode('live')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium rounded-md transition-all ${
                  viewportMode === 'live' ? 'bg-white/10 text-white' : (tab.running || liveViewBlocked) ? 'text-gray-700 cursor-not-allowed' : 'text-gray-500'
                }`}
                title={liveViewBlocked ? 'Live view blocked for this site' : tab.running ? 'Wait for actions to complete' : 'Live View'}
              >
                <Globe className="w-3 h-3" /> Live View
                {liveViewBlocked && <AlertTriangle className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-yellow-500" />}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setViewportMode('actions')}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-medium rounded-md transition-all ${
                  viewportMode === 'actions' ? 'bg-white/10 text-white' : 'text-gray-500'
                }`}
              >
                <Command className="w-3 h-3" /> Actions
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Progress */}
            {totalActions > 0 && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-3">
                <div className="w-24 h-1 bg-white/5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <motion.div
                    className="h-full bg-indigo-500 rounded-full"
                    initial={{ width: 0 }}
                    animate={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.3 }}
                  />
                </div>
                <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-500">{completedActions}/{totalActions}</span>
              </div>
            )}

            {tab.url && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 ml-auto">
                <Globe className="w-3 h-3 text-gray-600" />
                <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 font-mono truncate max-w-[260px]">{tab.url}</span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => window.open(tab.url, '_blank')}
                  className="text-gray-600 hover:text-white transition-colors"
                >
                  <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Viewport Content */}
          <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 relative">
            {viewportMode === 'live' ? (
              tab.url ? (
                <div className="h-full relative">
                  {liveViewBlocked ? (
                    <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-[#0a0a0f]">
                      <div className="text-center max-w-sm">
                        <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-red-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                          <Unplug className="w-8 h-8 text-red-400" />
                        </div>
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-300 mb-2">Live View Blocked</h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
                          {liveViewError || 'This site prevents iframe embedding for security reasons. Use Actions mode to see results.'}
                        </p>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 justify-center">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setViewportMode('actions')}
                            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white rounded-lg transition-colors"
                          >
                            Switch to Actions
                          </button>
                          <button
                            onClick={() => window.open(tab.url, '_blank')}
                            className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/15 text-gray-300 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                          >
                            Open in New Tab
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <iframe
                      ref={iframeRef}
                      key={tab.url}
                      src={tab.url}
                      className="w-full h-full border-0 bg-white"
                      sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups allow-popups-to-escape-sandbox"
                      title="Browser Viewport"
                      onError={() => {
                        setLiveViewBlocked(true)
                        setLiveViewError('Page failed to load in embedded view')
                        setViewportMode('actions')
                        setShowNotification('Could not provide live view, page was blocked')
                        setTimeout(() => setShowNotification(null), 3000)
                        toast({
                          title: 'Live View Error',
                          description: 'Page could not be displayed. Switched to Actions mode.',
                          variant: 'destructive',
                        })
                      }}
                      onLoad={() => {
                        // Try to detect if the page loaded an error message
                        try {
                          const iframeDoc = iframeRef.current?.contentDocument
                          if (iframeDoc) {
                            const bodyText = iframeDoc.body?.innerText || ''
                            const errorPatterns = [
                              "can't open this page",
                              'will not allow',
                              'refused to connect',
                              'blocked by',
                              'x-frame-options',
                            ]
                            if (errorPatterns.some(p => bodyText.toLowerCase().includes(p))) {
                              setLiveViewBlocked(true)
                              setLiveViewError('Page blocked iframe embedding')
                              setViewportMode('actions')
                              setShowNotification('Could not provide live view, page was blocked')
                              setTimeout(() => setShowNotification(null), 3000)
                            }
                          }
                        } catch {
                          // Cross-origin - can't read content, that's fine
                        }
                      }}
                    />
                  )}
                  {/* Loading overlay */}
                  {tab.running && (
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[#0a0a0f]/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-indigo-400">
                        <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium">Agent working...</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <div className="w-20 h-20 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500/10 to-indigo-500/10 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                      <Monitor className="w-10 h-10 text-blue-400/60" />
                    </div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-400 mb-1">Browser Viewport</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 max-w-sm">
                      When the agent navigates to a URL, the live page will render here. Some sites block embedding — Actions mode always works.
                    </p>
                    {tabs.length > 1 && (
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mt-4">
                        <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-600" />
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-600">{tabs.length} agents running</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            ) : (
              /* Actions Timeline View */
              <div className="h-full overflow-y-auto custom-scrollbar p-4">
                {tab.actions.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center">
                    <div className="text-center">
                      <Command className="w-10 h-10 text-gray-700 mx-auto mb-3" />
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">Actions will appear here as the agent executes them</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2 max-w-lg mx-auto">
                    {tab.actions.map((action, i) => (
                      <motion.div
                        key={action.id}
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                        transition={{ delay: i * 0.02 }}
                        className={`flex items-start gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border ${
                          action.status === 'running' ? 'bg-indigo-500/10 border-indigo-500/20' :
                          action.status === 'done' ? 'bg-emerald-500/5 border-emerald-500/10' :
                          action.status === 'error' ? 'bg-red-500/5 border-red-500/10' :
                          'bg-white/[0.02] border-white/[0.04]'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {/* Status Icon */}
                        <div className="mt-0.5">
                          {action.status === 'running' && <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-indigo-400" />}
                          {action.status === 'done' && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />}
                          {action.status === 'pending' && <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-gray-600" />}
                          {action.status === 'error' && <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-400" />}
                          {action.status === 'skipped' && <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-yellow-400" />}
                        </div>

                        {/* Action Details */}
                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-indigo-400">{action.type}</span>
                            {action.duration && (
                              <span className="text-[9px] text-gray-600">{action.duration}ms</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 truncate">{action.target}</p>
                          {action.value && (
                            <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 truncate mt-0.5">→ {action.value}</p>
                          )}
                          {action.result && action.status !== 'pending' && (
                            <p className={`text-[10px] mt-1 truncate ${action.status === 'error' ? 'text-red-400' : action.status === 'skipped' ? 'text-yellow-400' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>
                              {action.result}
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </motion.div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ──── Bottom-Right Notification ──── */}
      <AnimatePresence>
        {showNotification && (
          <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20, x: 0 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, x: 0 }}
            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
            className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 bg-red-500/90 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-lg shadow-lg backdrop-blur-sm"
          >
            <AlertTriangle className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            {showNotification}
          </motion.div>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </div>
  )
}


════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

DESCRIPTION: ROOT LAYOUT
LINES: 24

import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { Geist, Geist_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";

const geistSans = Geist({ variable: "--font-geist-sans", subsets: ["latin"] });
const geistMono = Geist_Mono({ variable: "--font-geist-mono", subsets: ["latin"] });

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Dextros Agent",
  description: "AI-powered browser agent with deep thinking and multi-agent orchestration.",
  icons: { icon: "data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 24 24' fill='none' stroke='%233B82F6' stroke-width='2' stroke-linecap='round' stroke-linejoin='round'><rect x='2' y='3' width='20' height='14' rx='2' ry='2'/><line x1='8' y1='21' x2='16' y2='21'/><line x1='12' y1='17' x2='12' y2='21'/></svg>" },
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <body className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable} antialiased bg-[#0a0a0f] text-gray-100`}>
        {children}
        <Toaster />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

@import "tw-animate-css";

@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));

@theme inline {
  --color-background: var(--background);
  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);
  --font-sans: var(--font-geist-sans);
  --font-mono: var(--font-geist-mono);
  --color-sidebar-ring: var(--sidebar-ring);
  --color-sidebar-border: var(--sidebar-border);
  --color-sidebar-accent-foreground: var(--sidebar-accent-foreground);
  --color-sidebar-accent: var(--sidebar-accent);
  --color-sidebar-primary-foreground: var(--sidebar-primary-foreground);
  --color-sidebar-primary: var(--sidebar-primary);
  --color-sidebar-foreground: var(--sidebar-foreground);
  --color-sidebar: var(--sidebar);
  --color-chart-5: var(--chart-5);
  --color-chart-4: var(--chart-4);
  --color-chart-3: var(--chart-3);
  --color-chart-2: var(--chart-2);
  --color-chart-1: var(--chart-1);
  --color-ring: var(--ring);
  --color-input: var(--input);
  --color-border: var(--border);
  --color-destructive: var(--destructive);
  --color-accent-foreground: var(--accent-foreground);
  --color-accent: var(--accent);
  --color-muted-foreground: var(--muted-foreground);
  --color-muted: var(--muted);
  --color-secondary-foreground: var(--secondary-foreground);
  --color-secondary: var(--secondary);
  --color-primary-foreground: var(--primary-foreground);
  --color-primary: var(--primary);
  --color-popover-foreground: var(--popover-foreground);
  --color-popover: var(--popover);
  --color-card-foreground: var(--card-foreground);
  --color-card: var(--card);
  --radius-sm: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  --radius-md: calc(var(--radius) - 2px);
  --radius-lg: var(--radius);
  --radius-xl: calc(var(--radius) + 4px);
}

:root {
  --radius: 0.625rem;
  --background: oklch(1 0 0);
  --foreground: oklch(0.145 0 0);
  --card: oklch(1 0 0);
  --card-foreground: oklch(0.145 0 0);
  --popover: oklch(1 0 0);
  --popover-foreground: oklch(0.145 0 0);
  --primary: oklch(0.205 0 0);
  --primary-foreground: oklch(0.985 0 0);
  --secondary: oklch(0.97 0 0);
  --secondary-foreground: oklch(0.205 0 0);
  --muted: oklch(0.97 0 0);
  --muted-foreground: oklch(0.556 0 0);
  --accent: oklch(0.97 0 0);
  --accent-foreground: oklch(0.205 0 0);
  --destructive: oklch(0.577 0.245 27.325);
  --border: oklch(0.922 0 0);
  --input: oklch(0.922 0 0);
  --ring: oklch(0.708 0 0);
  --chart-1: oklch(0.646 0.222 41.116);
  --chart-2: oklch(0.6 0.118 184.704);
