════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/works/page.tsx
DESCRIPTION: WORKS PAGE — Social app with friends/groups/chat
LINES: 1707
════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@/components/ui/card'
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogFooter,
  DialogClose,
} from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { ScrollArea } from '@/components/ui/scroll-area'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
  Users,
  MessageSquare,
  Code2,
  Plus,
  Send,
  Loader2,
  UserPlus,
  X,
  Trash2,
  Bot,
  Check,
  LogIn,
} from 'lucide-react'

/* ─── Types ─── */
interface User {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
}
interface FriendReq {
  id: string
  from?: User
  to?: User
  status: string
  createdAt: string
}
interface GroupMember {
  id: string
  userId: string
  role: string
  user: { id: string; name: string }
}
interface Group {
  id: string
  name: string
  isGroup: boolean
  members: GroupMember[]
  messages: Message[]
}
interface Message {
  id: string
  groupId: string
  userId: string | null
  content: string
  isAi: boolean
  createdAt: string
  user: { id: string; name: string } | null
}
interface Post {
  id: string
  userId: string
  title: string
  content: string
  language: string
  createdAt: string
  user: { id: string; name: string; email: string }
}

/* ─── Helpers ─── */
function timeAgo(dateStr: string) {
  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dateStr).getTime()
  const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000)
  if (mins < 1) return 'just now'
  if (mins < 60) return `${mins}m ago`
  const hrs = Math.floor(mins / 60)
  if (hrs < 24) return `${hrs}h ago`
  const days = Math.floor(hrs / 24)
  return `${days}d ago`
}

/* ─── Component ─── */
export default function WorksPage() {
  /* ── Auth ── */
  const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState<User | null>(null)
  const [authLoading, setAuthLoading] = useState(true)
  const [authMode, setAuthMode] = useState<'login' | 'signup'>('login')
  const [authEmail, setAuthEmail] = useState('')
  const [authName, setAuthName] = useState('')
  const [authPassword, setAuthPassword] = useState('')
  const [authSubmitting, setAuthSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [authError, setAuthError] = useState('')

  /* ── Sidebar ── */
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'chats' | 'myposts' | 'allposts'>('chats')

  /* ── Groups ── */
  const [groups, setGroups] = useState<Group[]>([])
  const [selectedGroup, setSelectedGroup] = useState<Group | null>(null)
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<Message[]>([])
  const [msgInput, setMsgInput] = useState('')
  const [sendingMsg, setSendingMsg] = useState(false)

  /* ── Posts ── */
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState<Post[]>([])
  const [selectedPost, setSelectedPost] = useState<Post | null>(null)
  const [postSearch, setPostSearch] = useState('')

  /* ── Friends ── */
  const [sentReqs, setSentReqs] = useState<FriendReq[]>([])
  const [recvReqs, setRecvReqs] = useState<FriendReq[]>([])
  const [friendsList, setFriendsList] = useState<FriendReq[]>([])

  /* ── Dialogs ── */
  const [showCreateGroup, setShowCreateGroup] = useState(false)
  const [showStartDm, setShowStartDm] = useState(false)
  const [showCreatePost, setShowCreatePost] = useState(false)
  const [showAddFriend, setShowAddFriend] = useState(false)

  /* ── Dialog Inputs ── */
  const [newGroupName, setNewGroupName] = useState('')
  const [dmFriendId, setDmFriendId] = useState('')
  const [newPostTitle, setNewPostTitle] = useState('')
  const [newPostContent, setNewPostContent] = useState('')
  const [newPostLang, setNewPostLang] = useState('text')
  const [friendEmail, setFriendEmail] = useState('')
  const [friendUsername, setFriendUsername] = useState('')

  /* ── Loading ── */
  const [creatingGroup, setCreatingGroup] = useState(false)
  const [creatingDm, setCreatingDm] = useState(false)
  const [creatingPost, setCreatingPost] = useState(false)
  const [addingFriend, setAddingFriend] = useState(false)

  /* ── Friends section expand ── */
  const [friendsExpanded, setFriendsExpanded] = useState(true)

  /* ── Refs ── */
  const messagesEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const msgInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     DATA LOADERS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  const loadGroups = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/groups')
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json()
        setGroups(data.groups || [])
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const loadMessages = async (groupId: string) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/groups/message?groupId=${groupId}`)
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json()
        setMessages(data.messages || [])
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const loadPosts = async (scope: 'mine' | 'all' = 'mine') => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/posts?scope=${scope}&search=${postSearch}`)
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json()
        setPosts(data.posts || [])
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  const loadFriends = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/friends')
      if (res.ok) {
        const data = await res.json()
        const sent: FriendReq[] = data.sent || []
        const recv: FriendReq[] = data.recv || []
        setSentReqs(sent.filter((f) => f.status === 'pending'))
        setRecvReqs(recv.filter((f) => f.status === 'pending'))
        setFriendsList(
          [
            ...sent.filter((f) => f.status === 'accepted'),
            ...recv.filter((f) => f.status === 'accepted'),
          ].sort(
            (a, b) =>
              new Date(b.createdAt).getTime() - new Date(a.createdAt).getTime()
          )
        )
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     EFFECTS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  // Auth check on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    ;(async () => {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/auth/me')
        if (res.ok) {
          const data = await res.json()
          if (data.user) {
            setCurrentUser(data.user)
          }
        }
      } catch {}
      setAuthLoading(false)
    })()
  }, [])

  const handleAuth = async () => {
    setAuthSubmitting(true)
    setAuthError('')
    try {
      const url = authMode === 'login' ? '/api/auth/login' : '/api/auth/signup'
      const body =
        authMode === 'login'
          ? { email: authEmail, password: authPassword }
          : { email: authEmail, name: authName, password: authPassword }
      const res = await fetch(url, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
      const data = await res.json()
      if (res.ok && data.user) {
        setCurrentUser(data.user)
      } else {
        setAuthError(data.error || 'Authentication failed')
      }
    } catch {
      setAuthError('Network error')
    } finally {
      setAuthSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  // Load data when authed
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!currentUser) return
    loadGroups()
    loadPosts('mine')
    loadFriends()
  }, [currentUser])

  // Load messages when group selected
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedGroup) {
      loadMessages(selectedGroup.id)
    }
  }, [selectedGroup])

  // Auto-scroll messages
  useEffect(() => {
    messagesEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
  }, [messages])

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     HANDLERS
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  const handleSendMessage = async () => {
    if (!msgInput.trim() || !selectedGroup || sendingMsg) return
    const content = msgInput.trim()
    const isAiCmd = content.startsWith('/ask ')
    setSendingMsg(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/groups/message', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          groupId: selectedGroup.id,
          content,
          isAi: isAiCmd,
        }),
      })
      if (res.ok) {
        setMsgInput('')
        await loadMessages(selectedGroup.id)
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setSendingMsg(false)
    }
  }

  const handleCreateGroup = async () => {
    if (!newGroupName.trim() || creatingGroup) return
    setCreatingGroup(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/groups', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: newGroupName.trim(), isGroup: true, memberIds: [] }),
      })
      if (res.ok) {
        setNewGroupName('')
        setShowCreateGroup(false)
        await loadGroups()
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setCreatingGroup(false)
    }
  }

  const handleStartDm = async () => {
    if (!dmFriendId || creatingDm) return
    setCreatingDm(true)
    try {
      const friend = friendsList.find((f) => {
        const other = f.from?.id === currentUser?.id ? f.to : f.from
        return other?.id === dmFriendId
      })
      const otherUser = friend
        ? friend.from?.id === currentUser?.id
          ? friend.to
          : friend.from
        : null
      const res = await fetch('/api/groups', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: `DM: ${otherUser?.name || 'User'}`,
          isGroup: false,
          memberIds: [dmFriendId],
        }),
      })
      if (res.ok) {
        setDmFriendId('')
        setShowStartDm(false)
        await loadGroups()
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setCreatingDm(false)
    }
  }

  const handleCreatePost = async () => {
    if (!newPostTitle.trim() || !newPostContent.trim() || creatingPost) return
    setCreatingPost(true)
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/posts', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          title: newPostTitle.trim(),
          content: newPostContent,
          language: newPostLang || 'text',
        }),
      })
      if (res.ok) {
        setNewPostTitle('')
        setNewPostContent('')
        setNewPostLang('text')
        setShowCreatePost(false)
        await loadPosts(activeTab === 'allposts' ? 'all' : 'mine')
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setCreatingPost(false)
    }
  }

  const handleAddFriend = async () => {
    if ((!friendEmail.trim() && !friendUsername.trim()) || addingFriend) return
    setAddingFriend(true)
    try {
      const body: any = {}
      if (friendUsername.trim()) body.username = friendUsername.trim()
      else body.email = friendEmail.trim()
      const res = await fetch('/api/friends', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      })
      if (res.ok) {
        setFriendEmail('')
        setFriendUsername('')
        setShowAddFriend(false)
        await loadFriends()
      } else {
        const data = await res.json()
        alert(data.error || 'Failed to send request')
      }
    } catch {
    } finally {
      setAddingFriend(false)
    }
  }

  const handleAcceptFriend = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/friends', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id, status: 'accepted' }),
      })
      await loadFriends()
    } catch {}
  }

  const handleRejectFriend = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/friends', {
        method: 'PATCH',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id, status: 'rejected' }),
      })
      await loadFriends()
    } catch {}
  }

  const handleRemoveFriend = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/friends', {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id }),
      })
      await loadFriends()
    } catch {}
  }

  const handleDeleteGroup = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/groups', {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id }),
      })
      if (selectedGroup?.id === id) {
        setSelectedGroup(null)
        setMessages([])
      }
      await loadGroups()
    } catch {}
  }

  const handleDeletePost = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await fetch('/api/posts', {
        method: 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ id }),
      })
      if (selectedPost?.id === id) setSelectedPost(null)
      await loadPosts(activeTab === 'allposts' ? 'all' : 'mine')
    } catch {}
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     AUTH LOADING
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  if (authLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-white">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
          className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3"
        >
          <Loader2 className="w-8 h-8 animate-spin text-gray-400" />
          <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm">Loading Works...</p>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!currentUser) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-50">
        <motion.div
          initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 20 }}
          animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
          className="w-full max-w-sm"
        >
          <Card className="border-gray-200 shadow-lg">
            <CardHeader className="text-center pb-4">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-gray-900 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3">
                <Users className="w-6 h-6 text-white" />
              </div>
              <CardTitle className="text-xl text-gray-900">Works</CardTitle>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
                Sign in to collaborate
              </p>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-3">
              <div className="flex rounded-lg bg-gray-100 p-0.5">
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setAuthMode('login'); setAuthError('') }}
                  className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium rounded-md transition-all ${
                    authMode === 'login'
                      ? 'bg-white text-gray-900 shadow-sm'
                      : 'text-gray-500'
                  }`}
                >
                  Sign In
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => { setAuthMode('signup'); setAuthError('') }}
                  className={`flex-1 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium rounded-md transition-all ${
                    authMode === 'signup'
                      ? 'bg-white text-gray-900 shadow-sm'
                      : 'text-gray-500'
                  }`}
                >
                  Sign Up
                </button>
              </div>

              {authMode === 'signup' && (
                <Input
                  value={authName}
                  onChange={(e) => setAuthName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Name"
                  className="bg-white border-gray-200"
                />
              )}
              <Input
                value={authEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setAuthEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Email"
                type="email"
                className="bg-white border-gray-200"
              />
              <Input
                value={authPassword}
                onChange={(e) => setAuthPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Password"
                type="password"
                className="bg-white border-gray-200"
                onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAuth() }}
              />

              {authError && (
                <p className="text-xs text-red-500 text-center">{authError}</p>
              )}

              <Button
                onClick={handleAuth}
                disabled={authSubmitting || !authEmail || !authPassword}
                className="w-full bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800"
              >
                {authSubmitting ? (
                  <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin mr-2" />
                ) : (
                  <LogIn className="w-4 h-4 mr-2" />
                )}
                {authMode === 'login' ? 'Sign In' : 'Create Account'}
              </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </motion.div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     DERIVED
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  const getFriendName = (f: FriendReq) => {
    if (f.from?.id === currentUser.id) return f.to?.name || 'Unknown'
    return f.from?.name || 'Unknown'
  }
  const getFriendEmail = (f: FriendReq) => {
    if (f.from?.id === currentUser.id) return f.to?.email || ''
    return f.from?.email || ''
  }
  const getFriendId = (f: FriendReq) => {
    if (f.from?.id === currentUser.id) return f.to?.id || ''
    return f.from?.id || ''
  }

  /* ══════════════════════════════════════════
     RENDER
     ══════════════════════════════════════════ */

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex bg-white">
      {/* ═══════ LEFT SIDEBAR ═══════ */}
      <aside className="w-72 border-r border-gray-200 flex flex-col bg-white shrink-0">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-200">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-gray-900 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-white" />
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">Online</h1>
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 ml-[42px]">
            {currentUser.name}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Tabs */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-gray-200">
          <button
            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('chats') }}
            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
              activeTab === 'chats'
                ? 'text-gray-900 border-b-2 border-gray-900'
                : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
            }`}
          >
            <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Chats
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('myposts'); loadPosts('mine') }}
            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
              activeTab === 'myposts'
                ? 'text-gray-900 border-b-2 border-gray-900'
                : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            My Posts
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => { setActiveTab('allposts'); loadPosts('all') }}
            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
              activeTab === 'allposts'
                ? 'text-gray-900 border-b-2 border-gray-900'
                : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Posts
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Tab Content */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-hidden">
          <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
            {activeTab === 'chats' ? (
              <motion.div
                key="chats"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 10 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.15 }}
                className="h-full flex flex-col"
              >
                {/* Actions */}
                <div className="p-3 flex gap-2">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    className="flex-1 text-xs h-8 border-gray-200"
                    onClick={() => setShowCreateGroup(true)}
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                    Group
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    className="flex-1 text-xs h-8 border-gray-200"
                    onClick={() => setShowStartDm(true)}
                  >
                    <MessageSquare className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                    DM
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {/* Group List */}
                <ScrollArea className="flex-1 custom-scrollbar">
                  <div className="px-2 pb-2 space-y-0.5">
                    {groups.length === 0 && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-6">
                        No conversations yet
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {groups.map((g) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedGroup?.id === g.id
                      const lastMsg = g.messages?.[0]
                      return (
                        <motion.button
                          key={g.id}
                          whileHover={{ backgroundColor: 'rgb(249,250,251)' }}
                          whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedGroup(g)
                            setSelectedPost(null)
                          }}
                          className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors group relative ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-gray-100 ring-1 ring-gray-200'
                              : 'hover:bg-gray-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <div
                              className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                                g.isGroup
                                  ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-600'
                                  : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {g.isGroup ? (
                                <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              ) : (
                                <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 truncate">
                                {g.name}
                              </p>
                              {lastMsg && (
                                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                                  {lastMsg.isAi && '🤖 '}
                                  {lastMsg.content}
                                </p>
                              )}
                            </div>
                            <button
                              onClick={(e) => {
                                e.stopPropagation()
                                handleDeleteGroup(g.id)
                              }}
                              className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-1 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded transition-all"
                            >
                              <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3 text-gray-400" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </motion.button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </ScrollArea>
              </motion.div>
            ) : activeTab === 'myposts' ? (
              <motion.div
                key="myposts"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 10 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.15 }}
                className="h-full flex flex-col"
              >
                {/* Search + Create Post */}
                <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
                  <div className="relative">
                    <svg className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z" /></svg>
                    <Input placeholder="Search my posts..." value={postSearch} onChange={(e) => { setPostSearch(e.target.value); loadPosts('mine') }} className="h-8 pl-9 text-xs border-gray-200" />
                  </div>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    className="w-full text-xs h-8 border-gray-200"
                    onClick={() => setShowCreatePost(true)}
                  >
                    <Plus className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                    New Post
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {/* Posts List */}
                <ScrollArea className="flex-1 custom-scrollbar">
                  <div className="px-2 pb-2 space-y-0.5">
                    {posts.length === 0 && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-6">
                        No posts yet
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {posts.map((p) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedPost?.id === p.id
                      return (
                        <motion.button
                          key={p.id}
                          whileHover={{ backgroundColor: 'rgb(249,250,251)' }}
                          whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedPost(p)
                            setSelectedGroup(null)
                          }}
                          className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors group relative ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-gray-100 ring-1 ring-gray-200'
                              : 'hover:bg-gray-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-violet-100 text-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                              <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 truncate">
                                {p.title}
                              </p>
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                                <Badge
                                  variant="secondary"
                                  className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 h-4 bg-gray-100 text-gray-500"
                                >
                                  {p.language}
                                </Badge>
                                <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
                                  {p.user?.name}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            {p.userId === currentUser.id && (
                              <button
                                onClick={(e) => {
                                  e.stopPropagation()
                                  handleDeletePost(p.id)
                                }}
                                className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-1 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded transition-all"
                              >
                                <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3 text-gray-400" />
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </motion.button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </ScrollArea>
              </motion.div>
            ) : (
              <motion.div
                key="allposts"
                initial={{ opacity: 0, x: -10 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, x: 0 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0, x: 10 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.15 }}
                className="h-full flex flex-col"
              >
                {/* Search */}
                <div className="p-3">
                  <div className="relative">
                    <svg className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-400 absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor"><path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M21 21l-6-6m2-5a7 7 0 11-14 0 7 7 0 0114 0z" /></svg>
                    <Input placeholder="Search all posts..." value={postSearch} onChange={(e) => { setPostSearch(e.target.value); loadPosts('all') }} className="h-8 pl-9 text-xs border-gray-200" />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* All Posts List */}
                <ScrollArea className="flex-1 custom-scrollbar">
                  <div className="px-2 pb-2 space-y-0.5">
                    {posts.length === 0 && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 text-center py-6">
                        No posts found
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {posts.map((p) => {
                      const isSelected = selectedPost?.id === p.id
                      return (
                        <motion.button
                          key={p.id}
                          whileHover={{ backgroundColor: 'rgb(249,250,251)' }}
                          whileTap={{ scale: 0.98 }}
                          onClick={() => {
                            setSelectedPost(p)
                            setSelectedGroup(null)
                          }}
                          className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg transition-colors group relative ${
                            isSelected
                              ? 'bg-gray-100 ring-1 ring-gray-200'
                              : 'hover:bg-gray-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                            <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-violet-100 text-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5">
                              <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900 truncate">
                                {p.title}
                              </p>
                              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-0.5">
                                <Badge
                                  variant="secondary"
                                  className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0 h-4 bg-gray-100 text-gray-500"
                                >
                                  {p.language}
                                </Badge>
                                <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">
                                  {p.user?.name}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </motion.button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </ScrollArea>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>

        {/* ═══════ FRIENDS SECTION ═══════ */}
        <div className="border-t border-gray-200">
          <button
            onClick={() => setFriendsExpanded(!friendsExpanded)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-gray-500" />
              <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
                Friends
              </span>
              {(recvReqs.length > 0 || friendsList.length > 0) && (
                <span className="text-[10px] bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 font-medium">
                  {friendsList.length}
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
            <motion.div
              animate={{ rotate: friendsExpanded ? 180 : 0 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
            >
              <svg
                className="w-3 h-3 text-gray-400"
                fill="none"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                stroke="currentColor"
              >
                <path
                  strokeLinecap="round"
                  strokeLinejoin="round"
                  strokeWidth={2}
                  d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"
                />
              </svg>
            </motion.div>
          </button>

          <AnimatePresence>
            {friendsExpanded && (
              <motion.div
                initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ height: 'auto', opacity: 1 }}
                exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                className="overflow-hidden"
              >
                <div className="px-3 pb-3">
                  {/* Add Friend */}
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="sm"
                    className="w-full text-xs h-7 text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 mb-2"
                    onClick={() => setShowAddFriend(true)}
                  >
                    <UserPlus className="w-3 h-3 mr-1.5" />
                    Add Friend
                  </Button>

                  {/* Pending Requests */}
                  {recvReqs.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mb-2">
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold px-1 mb-1">
                        Pending Requests
                      </p>
                      {recvReqs.map((r) => (
                        <div
                          key={r.id}
                          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50 group"
                        >
                          <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-amber-100 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                            <UserPlus className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-700 truncate flex-1">
                            {r.from?.name || 'Unknown'}
                          </p>
                          <div className="flex gap-1">
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleAcceptFriend(r.id)}
                              className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600"
                            >
                              <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
                            </button>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleRejectFriend(r.id)}
                              className="p-1 rounded hover:bg-red-100 text-red-500"
                            >
                              <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Sent Requests */}
                  {sentReqs.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mb-2">
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold px-1 mb-1">
                        Sent Requests
                      </p>
                      {sentReqs.map((r) => (
                        <div
                          key={r.id}
                          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50"
                        >
                          <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                            <Users className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                          </div>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate flex-1">
                            {r.to?.name || 'Unknown'}
                          </p>
                          <Badge
                            variant="secondary"
                            className="text-[9px] px-1 h-4 bg-gray-100 text-gray-400"
                          >
                            pending
                          </Badge>
                        </div>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Friends List */}
                  <div>
                    {friendsList.length > 0 && (
                      <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold px-1 mb-1">
                        Your Friends
                      </p>
                    )}
                    <ScrollArea className="max-h-32 custom-scrollbar">
                      <div className="space-y-0.5">
                        {friendsList.map((f) => (
                          <div
                            key={f.id}
                            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-2 py-1.5 rounded-md hover:bg-gray-50 group"
                          >
                            <div className="w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                              <span className="text-[9px] font-bold">
                                {getFriendName(f).charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                              <p className="text-xs text-gray-700 truncate">
                                {getFriendName(f)}
                              </p>
                            </div>
                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleRemoveFriend(f.id)}
                              className="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 p-0.5 rounded hover:bg-red-100 text-red-400 transition-all"
                            >
                              <X className="w-2.5 h-2.5" />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </ScrollArea>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </motion.div>
            )}
          </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
      </aside>

      {/* ═══════ MAIN CONTENT ═══════ */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0">
        <AnimatePresence mode="wait">
          {/* ─── CHAT VIEW ─── */}
          {selectedGroup ? (
            <motion.div
              key={`chat-${selectedGroup.id}`}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
              className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0"
            >
              {/* Chat Header */}
              <div className="px-6 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div
                    className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center ${
                      selectedGroup.isGroup
                        ? 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600'
                        : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {selectedGroup.isGroup ? (
                      <Users className="w-4 h-4" />
                    ) : (
                      <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4" />
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">
                      {selectedGroup.name}
                    </h2>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                      {selectedGroup.isGroup
                        ? `${selectedGroup.members?.length || 0} members`
                        : 'Direct Message'}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {selectedGroup.members?.map((m) => (
                    <div
                      key={m.id}
                      className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-gray-200 flex items-center justify-center"
                      title={m.user?.name}
                    >
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-medium text-gray-600">
                        {m.user?.name?.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Messages */}
              <ScrollArea className="flex-1 custom-scrollbar">
                <div className="px-6 py-4 space-y-3">
                  {messages.length === 0 && (
                    <div className="text-center py-16">
                      <MessageSquare className="w-10 h-10 text-gray-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                        No messages yet. Start the conversation!
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 mt-1">
                        Use <code className="bg-gray-100 px-1 py-0.5 rounded text-violet-600">/ask</code> to query AI
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}
                  {messages.map((msg, i) => {
                    const isOwn = msg.userId === currentUser.id
                    const isAiQuery = msg.isAi || msg.content.startsWith('/ask ')
                    const showAvatar =
                      i === 0 || messages[i - 1]?.userId !== msg.userId

                    return (
                      <motion.div
                        key={msg.id}
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 5 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                        transition={{ delay: i * 0.02, duration: 0.2 }}
                        className={`flex ${
                          isOwn ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div
                          className={`flex gap-2 max-w-[70%] ${
                            isOwn ? 'flex-row-reverse' : ''
                          }`}
                        >
                          {/* Avatar */}
                          {showAvatar && !isOwn ? (
                            <div
                              className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${
                                isAiQuery
                                  ? 'bg-violet-100 text-violet-600'
                                  : 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-600'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {isAiQuery ? (
                                <Bot className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              ) : (
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold">
                                  {msg.user?.name?.charAt(0).toUpperCase() ||
                                    '?'}
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </div>
                          ) : !isOwn ? (
                            <div className="w-7 shrink-0" />
                          ) : null}

                          {/* Message Bubble */}
                          <div>
                            {showAvatar && (
                              <div
                                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1 ${
                                  isOwn ? 'justify-end' : ''
                                }`}
                              >
                                <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-gray-500">
                                  {isAiQuery
                                    ? 'AI Query'
                                    : isOwn
                                    ? 'You'
                                    : msg.user?.name || 'Unknown'}
                                </span>
                                <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-300">
                                  {timeAgo(msg.createdAt)}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            )}
                            <div
                              className={`rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2 text-sm leading-relaxed ${
                                isAiQuery
                                  ? 'bg-violet-50 text-violet-900 border border-violet-200'
                                  : isOwn
                                  ? 'bg-gray-900 text-white'
                                  : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-800'
                              }`}
                            >
                              {isAiQuery && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5">
                                  <Bot
                                    className={`w-3 h-3 ${
                                      isOwn
                                        ? 'text-violet-300'
                                        : 'text-violet-500'
                                    }`}
                                  />
                                  <Badge className="text-[9px] h-4 px-1.5 bg-violet-200 text-violet-700 hover:bg-violet-200 border-0">
                                    Asked AI
                                  </Badge>
                                </div>
                              )}
                              <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">
                                {msg.content}
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </motion.div>
                    )
                  })}
                  <div ref={messagesEndRef} />
                </div>
              </ScrollArea>

              {/* Message Input */}
              <div className="px-6 py-3 border-t border-gray-200 bg-white">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Input
                    ref={msgInputRef}
                    value={msgInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setMsgInput(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => {
                      if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
                        e.preventDefault()
                        handleSendMessage()
                      }
                    }}
                    placeholder="Type a message... (/ask to query AI)"
                    className="flex-1 h-10 bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 text-sm focus:bg-white"
                    disabled={sendingMsg}
                  />
                  <Button
                    onClick={handleSendMessage}
                    disabled={!msgInput.trim() || sendingMsg}
                    size="icon"
                    className="h-10 w-10 bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800 rounded-xl shrink-0"
                  >
                    {sendingMsg ? (
                      <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                    ) : (
                      <Send className="w-4 h-4" />
                    )}
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          ) : selectedPost ? (
            /* ─── POST VIEW ─── */
            <motion.div
              key={`post-${selectedPost.id}`}
              initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
              transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
              className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-h-0"
            >
              {/* Post Header */}
              <div className="px-6 py-3 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-violet-100 text-violet-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <Code2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-900">
                      {selectedPost.title}
                    </h2>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-0.5">
                      <Badge className="text-[10px] h-4 px-1.5 bg-violet-100 text-violet-700 hover:bg-violet-100 border-0">
                        {selectedPost.language}
                      </Badge>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                        by {selectedPost.user?.name || 'Unknown'} •{' '}
                        {timeAgo(selectedPost.createdAt)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  {selectedPost.userId === currentUser.id && (
                    <Button
                      variant="ghost"
                      size="sm"
                      className="text-red-500 hover:text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 h-8"
                      onClick={() => handleDeletePost(selectedPost.id)}
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" />
                      Delete
                    </Button>
                  )}
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="sm"
                    className="h-8 text-gray-500"
                    onClick={() => setSelectedPost(null)}
                  >
                    <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1" />
                    Close
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Code Content */}
              <ScrollArea className="flex-1 custom-scrollbar">
                <div className="p-6">
                  <div className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="bg-gray-50 px-4 py-2 border-b border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                          <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-400" />
                          <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400" />
                          <div className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-400" />
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 ml-2">
                          {selectedPost.language}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <Button
                        variant="ghost"
                        size="sm"
                        className="h-6 text-[10px] text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"
                        onClick={() =>
                          navigator.clipboard.writeText(selectedPost.content)
                        }
                      >
                        Copy
                      </Button>
                    </div>
                    <pre className="p-4 text-sm text-gray-800 font-mono overflow-x-auto bg-white leading-relaxed">
                      <code>{selectedPost.content}</code>
                    </pre>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </ScrollArea>
            </motion.div>
          ) : (
            /* ─── DEFAULT / FEED VIEW ─── */
            <motion.div
              key="default"
              initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
              animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
              exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
              className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center"
            >
              <div className="text-center max-w-md">
                <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                  <Users className="w-7 h-7 text-gray-400" />
                </div>
                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-1">
                  Welcome to Works
                </h2>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-6">
                  Select a conversation to start chatting, or browse posts from
                  the community.
                </p>
                <div className="flex gap-3 justify-center">
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    className="border-gray-200"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setActiveTab('chats')
                      if (groups.length === 0) setShowCreateGroup(true)
                    }}
                  >
                    <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
                    Start Chatting
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    variant="outline"
                    size="sm"
                    className="border-gray-200"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setActiveTab('myposts')
                      if (posts.length === 0) setShowCreatePost(true)
                    }}
                  >
                    <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
                    Share Code
                  </Button>
                </div>

                {/* Quick Stats */}
                <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                      {groups.length}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Chats
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                      {posts.length}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Posts
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                      {friendsList.length}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Friends
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </motion.div>
          )}
        </AnimatePresence>
      </main>

      {/* ═══════ DIALOGS ═══════ */}

      {/* Create Group */}
      <Dialog open={showCreateGroup} onOpenChange={setShowCreateGroup}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-white border-gray-200">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-gray-900">Create Group</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <Input
              value={newGroupName}
              onChange={(e) => setNewGroupName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Group name"
              className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200"
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                if (e.key === 'Enter') handleCreateGroup()
              }}
            />
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
              You can add more members after creating the group.
            </p>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <DialogClose asChild>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="border-gray-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
            </DialogClose>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleCreateGroup}
              disabled={!newGroupName.trim() || creatingGroup}
              className="bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              {creatingGroup ? (
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
              ) : (
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
              )}
              Create
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Start DM */}
      <Dialog open={showStartDm} onOpenChange={setShowStartDm}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-white border-gray-200">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-gray-900">
              Start Direct Message
            </DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            {friendsList.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 py-4 text-center">
                No friends yet. Add friends first to start a DM.
              </p>
            ) : (
              <ScrollArea className="max-h-48 custom-scrollbar">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  {friendsList.map((f) => (
                    <button
                      key={f.id}
                      onClick={() => setDmFriendId(getFriendId(f))}
                      className={`w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg transition-colors ${
                        dmFriendId === getFriendId(f)
                          ? 'bg-gray-100 ring-1 ring-gray-200'
                          : 'hover:bg-gray-50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">
                          {getFriendName(f).charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-left">
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-900">
                          {getFriendName(f)}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                          {getFriendEmail(f)}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </ScrollArea>
            )}
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <DialogClose asChild>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="border-gray-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
            </DialogClose>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleStartDm}
              disabled={!dmFriendId || creatingDm}
              className="bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              {creatingDm ? (
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
              ) : (
                <MessageSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
              )}
              Start DM
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Create Post */}
      <Dialog open={showCreatePost} onOpenChange={setShowCreatePost}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-white border-gray-200 sm:max-w-lg">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-gray-900">Share Code</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <Input
              value={newPostTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPostTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Title"
              className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200"
            />
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Input
                value={newPostLang}
                onChange={(e) => setNewPostLang(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Language"
                className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 w-32"
              />
              <div className="flex gap-1.5 flex-wrap items-center">
                {['javascript', 'python', 'typescript', 'rust', 'go', 'sql'].map(
                  (lang) => (
                    <button
                      key={lang}
                      onClick={() => setNewPostLang(lang)}
                      className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded-md transition-colors ${
                        newPostLang === lang
                          ? 'bg-gray-900 text-white'
                          : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {lang}
                    </button>
                  )
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
            <Textarea
              value={newPostContent}
              onChange={(e) => setNewPostContent(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Paste your code or content here..."
              className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200 font-mono text-sm min-h-[160px] resize-y"
            />
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <DialogClose asChild>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="border-gray-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
            </DialogClose>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleCreatePost}
              disabled={
                !newPostTitle.trim() || !newPostContent.trim() || creatingPost
              }
              className="bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              {creatingPost ? (
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
              ) : (
                <Code2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
              )}
              Share
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>

      {/* Add Friend */}
      <Dialog open={showAddFriend} onOpenChange={setShowAddFriend}>
        <DialogContent className="bg-white border-gray-200">
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle className="text-gray-900">Add Friend</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-3 py-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Username</label>
              <Input
                value={friendUsername}
                onChange={(e) => setFriendUsername(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter username"
                className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200"
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAddFriend()
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <div className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400">or</div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
              <Input
                value={friendEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setFriendEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Enter email address"
                type="email"
                className="bg-gray-50 border-gray-200"
                onKeyDown={(e) => {
                  if (e.key === 'Enter') handleAddFriend()
                }}
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">
              Send a friend request to connect and start collaborating.
            </p>
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <DialogClose asChild>
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                size="sm"
                className="border-gray-200"
              >
                Cancel
              </Button>
            </DialogClose>
            <Button
              size="sm"
              onClick={handleAddFriend}
              disabled={(!friendEmail.trim() && !friendUsername.trim()) || addingFriend}
              className="bg-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-800"
            >
              {addingFriend ? (
                <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin mr-1.5" />
              ) : (
                <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mr-1.5" />
              )}
              Send Request
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  )
}


════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
FILE: src/app/api/browser-agent/route.ts
DESCRIPTION: BROWSER AGENT API — Real browser automation via agent-browser CLI + AI vision fallback

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { execFile } from 'child_process'
import { promisify } from 'util'

const execFileAsync = promisify(execFile)

// Advanced Browser Agent API — executes browser actions with REAL browser automation
// Uses agent-browser CLI (Rust-based headless Chrome) for REAL clicking, typing, etc.
// Falls back to fetch + AI vision when agent-browser is not available

let zaiInstance: any = null
async function getZAI() {
  if (!zaiInstance) {
    const ZAI = (await import('z-ai-web-dev-sdk')).default
    zaiInstance = await ZAI.create()
  }
  return zaiInstance
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AGENT-BROWSER CLI — Real headless browser automation
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION = 'dextros-session'

interface AgentBrowserResult {
  success: boolean
  output: string
  error?: string
}

async function agentBrowser(...args: string[]): Promise<AgentBrowserResult> {
  try {
    const allArgs = ['--session', AGENT_BROWSER_SESSION, ...args]
    const { stdout, stderr } = await execFileAsync('agent-browser', allArgs, {
      timeout: 30000,
      maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024, // 1MB buffer
    })
    return { success: true, output: (stdout || '').trim() }
  } catch (e: any) {
    return { success: false, output: (e.stdout || '').trim(), error: (e.stderr || e.message || '').trim() }
  }
}

// Cached snapshot of interactive elements from agent-browser
let cachedSnapshotElements: { ref: string; role: string; name: string; type?: string }[] = []

async function agentBrowserSnapshot(): Promise<{ ref: string; role: string; name: string; type?: string }[]> {
  const result = await agentBrowser('snapshot', '-i')
  if (!result.success) {
    console.log('agentBrowserSnapshot failed:', result.error?.slice(0, 200))
    return []
  }
  
  const elements: { ref: string; role: string; name: string; type?: string }[] = []
  // Parse the snapshot output - format is like:
  // - textbox "Email or Phone Number" [required, ref=e4]
  // - button "Log In" [ref=e5]
  // - heading "Welcome back!" [level=1, ref=e3]
  const lines = result.output.split('\n')
  for (const line of lines) {
    // Match [ref=eN] format (actual agent-browser output format)
    const refMatch = line.match(/ref=(e\d+)/)
    if (!refMatch) continue
    const ref = `@${refMatch[1]}`
    
    // Extract role — first word before the quoted name, e.g. "textbox", "button", "heading"
    // Format: "  - textbox "Email" [ref=e4]" or "- generic "stuff" [ref=e1]"
    const roleMatch = line.match(/-\s+(\w+)/)
    const role = roleMatch ? roleMatch[1] : ''
    
    // Extract name/text — the first quoted string
    const nameMatch = line.match(/"([^"]+)"/)
    const name = nameMatch ? nameMatch[1] : ''
    
    // Extract type attribute
    const typeMatch = line.match(/type="([^"]+)"/)
    const type = typeMatch ? typeMatch[1] : undefined
    
    elements.push({ ref, role, name, type })
  }
  
  console.log(`agentBrowserSnapshot parsed ${elements.length} elements from ${lines.length} lines`)
  cachedSnapshotElements = elements
  return elements
}

// Find an element by description in the cached snapshot
function findElementInSnapshot(description: string): { ref: string; role: string; name: string } | null {
  const desc = description.toLowerCase()
  
  if (cachedSnapshotElements.length === 0) return null
  
  // Direct name match
  let match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => e.name.toLowerCase().includes(desc))
  if (match) return match
  
  // Role-based matching
  if (desc.includes('login') || desc.includes('log in') || desc.includes('submit')) {
    // PRIORITY: Login/submit buttons should match before other buttons
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.role === 'button' && (e.name.toLowerCase().includes('log in') || e.name.toLowerCase().includes('login') || e.name.toLowerCase().includes('submit') || e.name.toLowerCase() === 'log in')
    )
    if (match) return match
    // Also try matching just "log" in button name
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.role === 'button' && e.name.toLowerCase().includes('log')
    )
    if (match) return match
  }
  if (desc.includes('button')) {
    const buttonDesc = desc.replace('button', '').trim()
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => e.role === 'button' && e.name.toLowerCase().includes(buttonDesc))
    if (match) return match
  }
  if (desc.includes('input') || desc.includes('field') || desc.includes('textbox')) {
    // For "email input", find the FIRST textbox; for "password input", find the SECOND
    const textboxes = cachedSnapshotElements.filter(e => e.role === 'textbox' || e.role === 'searchbox' || e.type === 'text' || e.type === 'email' || e.type === 'password')
    if (desc.includes('email') && textboxes.length > 0) {
      // Prefer textbox whose name includes "email" or "phone"
      match = textboxes.find(e => e.name.toLowerCase().includes('email') || e.name.toLowerCase().includes('phone') || e.type === 'email')
      if (match) return match
      // Fallback: first textbox
      return textboxes[0]
    }
    if (desc.includes('password') && textboxes.length > 1) {
      // Prefer textbox whose name includes "password" or type=password
      match = textboxes.find(e => e.name.toLowerCase().includes('password') || e.type === 'password')
      if (match) return match
      // Fallback: second textbox (first is usually email)
      return textboxes[1]
    }
    if (textboxes.length > 0) return textboxes[0]
  }
  if (desc.includes('email')) {
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.name.toLowerCase().includes('email') || 
      e.name.toLowerCase().includes('phone') ||
      e.type === 'email'
    )
    if (match) return match
    // Fallback: first textbox
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => e.role === 'textbox')
    if (match) return match
  }
  if (desc.includes('password')) {
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.name.toLowerCase().includes('password') || 
      e.type === 'password'
    )
    if (match) return match
    // Fallback: second textbox
    const textboxes = cachedSnapshotElements.filter(e => e.role === 'textbox')
    if (textboxes.length > 1) return textboxes[1]
  }
  if (desc.includes('send')) {
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.role === 'button' && (e.name.toLowerCase().includes('send') || e.name.toLowerCase().includes('submit'))
    )
    if (match) return match
  }
  if (desc.includes('message') || desc.includes('chat') || desc.includes('text')) {
    // For message/chat input, find the LAST textbox (not the first which might be search)
    const textboxes = cachedSnapshotElements.filter(e => e.role === 'textbox' || e.role === 'combobox' || e.type === 'text')
    if (textboxes.length > 0) return textboxes[textboxes.length - 1]
  }
  if (desc.includes('search')) {
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => 
      e.role === 'searchbox' || e.name.toLowerCase().includes('search')
    )
    if (match) return match
  }
  
  // Fuzzy match: look for any element whose name contains any word from the description
  const words = desc.split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length > 2)
  for (const word of words) {
    match = cachedSnapshotElements.find(e => e.name.toLowerCase().includes(word))
    if (match) return match
  }
  
  return null
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// REAL WEB FETCHER — actually gets page content from the internet
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

interface FetchResult {
  success: boolean
  html?: string
  text?: string
  title?: string
  url?: string
  status?: number
  error?: string
  headers?: Record<string, string>
}

async function fetchPage(url: string): Promise<FetchResult> {
  try {
    const controller = new AbortController()
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 15000)
    
    const res = await fetch(url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
        'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
        'Accept-Language': 'en-US,en;q=0.9',
        'Cache-Control': 'no-cache',
      },
      redirect: 'follow',
    })
    clearTimeout(timeout)

    const html = await res.text()
    const title = html.match(/<title[^>]*>(.*?)<\/title>/i)?.[1] || ''
    
    // Strip HTML to get readable text
    let text = html
      .replace(/<script[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi, '')
      .replace(/<style[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi, '')
      .replace(/<noscript[\s\S]*?<\/noscript>/gi, '')
      .replace(/<!--[\s\S]*?-->/g, '')
      .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
      .replace(/&amp;/g, '&').replace(/&lt;/g, '<').replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
      .replace(/&quot;/g, '"').replace(/&#39;/g, "'").replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim()
    
    // Truncate to reasonable size
    if (text.length > 15000) text = text.slice(0, 15000) + '... [truncated]'

    return {
      success: true,
      html: html.length > 50000 ? html.slice(0, 50000) + '... [truncated]' : html,
      text,
      title: title.trim(),
      url: res.url || url,
      status: res.status,
      headers: Object.fromEntries(res.headers.entries()),
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return {
      success: false,
      error: err.name === 'AbortError' ? 'Request timed out (15s)' : err.message || 'Fetch failed',
    }
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AI VISION — actually SEE screenshots with GLM-4V
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function fetchScreenshotAsBase64(screenshotUrl: string): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(screenshotUrl, {
      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(15000),
      headers: { 'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36' },
    })
    if (!res.ok) return null
    const buffer = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer())
    return `data:image/png;base64,${buffer.toString('base64')}`
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

// Retry wrapper for API calls with rate limit handling
async function callWithRetry(fn: () => Promise<any>, maxRetries: number = 2, baseDelay: number = 2000): Promise<any> {
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= maxRetries; attempt++) {
    try {
      return await fn()
    } catch (e: any) {
      const isRateLimit = e.message?.includes('429') || e.message?.includes('rate') || e.message?.includes('too many')
      if (isRateLimit && attempt < maxRetries) {
        const delay = baseDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt)
        console.log(`Rate limited, retrying in ${delay}ms (attempt ${attempt + 1}/${maxRetries})`)
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, delay))
        continue
      }
      throw e
    }
  }
}

// Known site layouts — hardcoded element positions for common SPAs
function getKnownSiteElements(pageUrl: string): any[] | null {
  try {
    const url = new URL(pageUrl)
    // Discord login page
    if ((url.hostname === 'discord.com' && url.pathname.includes('login')) || url.hostname === 'discord.gg') {
      return [
        { type: 'input', label: 'Email or Phone Number', x: 640, y: 310, width: 380, height: 44, selector: "input[name='email']", action: 'Enter email address' },
        { type: 'input', label: 'Password', x: 640, y: 390, width: 380, height: 44, selector: "input[name='password']", action: 'Enter password' },
        { type: 'button', label: 'Log In', x: 640, y: 460, width: 380, height: 44, selector: "button[type='submit']", action: 'Submit login form' },
        { type: 'link', label: 'Register', x: 640, y: 520, width: 100, height: 20, selector: "a[href='/register']", action: 'Go to registration page' },
        { type: 'link', label: 'Forgot Password', x: 640, y: 550, width: 150, height: 20, selector: "a[href*='reset']", action: 'Reset password' },
      ]
    }
    // Discord main page (after login)
    if (url.hostname === 'discord.com' && !url.pathname.includes('login')) {
      return [
        { type: 'input', label: 'Search', x: 340, y: 50, width: 400, height: 36, selector: "input[aria-label='Search']", action: 'Search Discord' },
        { type: 'button', label: 'Home', x: 50, y: 50, width: 48, height: 48, selector: "[class*='homeIcon']", action: 'Go to home' },
      ]
    }
  } catch {}
  return null
}

async function analyzeScreenshotWithVision(
  screenshotUrl: string,
  prompt: string,
  pageUrl: string = '',
  pageContent: string = ''
): Promise<string> {
  const contextText = pageContent ? `\n\nPage URL: ${pageUrl}\nPage text content (may be empty for SPAs): ${pageContent.slice(0, 2000)}` : `\n\nPage URL: ${pageUrl}`

  // Step 0: Try agent-browser screenshot (real browser, most reliable)
  try {
    const screenshotPath = `/tmp/agent-vision-${Date.now()}.png`
    const screenshotResult = await agentBrowser('screenshot', screenshotPath)
    if (screenshotResult.success) {
      // Read the screenshot as base64
      const fs = await import('fs/promises')
      try {
        const imageBuffer = await fs.readFile(screenshotPath)
        const base64Image = `data:image/png;base64,${imageBuffer.toString('base64')}`
        const zai = await getZAI()
        
        const completion = await callWithRetry(() => zai.chat.completions.create({
